LoadingScreen/Help00=You gain Boons at level 1, 4, 7, and 10, and Curses every enemy talent tier.
LoadingScreen/Help01=Completing map objectives replaces a future Curse with a Boon, once per game.
LoadingScreen/Help02=Some Curses are more powerful than others, choose carefully!
LoadingScreen/Help03=Certain Boons and Curses appear only on specific maps or Heroes.
LoadingScreen/Help04=Boons and Curses can stack their effects and become significantly more powerful.
LoadingScreen/Help05=Heroes with a golden border have unique Boons which only appear at the start of the run.
LoadingScreen/Help06=You can reroll Boons or Curses you don't like.
LoadingScreen/Help07=You gain a powerful Legendary Boon every 4 games.
LoadingScreen/Help08=You can ban Boons or Curses, preventing them from rolling again for the rest of the run.
LoadingScreen/Help09=You gain a Milestone Boon with unique effects on Round 7. 
LoadingScreen/Help10=Boons and curses up to level 10 are obtained next game when skipped.
LoadingScreen/Help11=Challenge curses are difficult to deal with but offer great rewards.
LoadingScreen/Help12=Enemy stats scale over time on Heroic difficulty or higher.
LoadingScreen/Help12=You win by getting to Round 14 and destroying the enemy Core! You can stop or continue into an endless run afterwards.
LoadingScreen/Help13=Curses will no longer swap to boons after Round 14 when completing objectives on Mythic difficulty.

UI/MapLoadingScreen/LibAffx/Description1=Gain powerful, permanent Boons throughout your run to increase your and your team's strength!
UI/MapLoadingScreen/LibAffx/Description2=The game becomes more difficult over time as you obtain dangerous Curses.
UI/MapLoadingScreen/LibAffx/Description3=Victory is not the end. Continue on to a new map, keeping your Boons and Curses, and try to get to Round 14 and win!

Text/ExcludeChoGall=<n/><n/><c val="FFDE32">Does not apply to Cho'Gall.</c>
Text/Information=Welcome to Heroes Rogue! This is a singleplayer rogue-like mod for Heroes of the Storm. Throughout the game, you will have to choose <c val="#TooltipNumbers">Boons</c> providing various permanent buffs and <c val="c52020">Curses</c> which make the game more difficult. Your boons and curses are automatically saved after destroying the enemy Core, carrying them over into the next game. The game can be quit and played at another time, and will resume where you left off. You win by getting to round <c val="#TooltipNumbers">14</c>! If your Core gets destroyed, the run ends and all boons and curses are reset.<n/><n/>The following Heroes have special interactions with boons and curses:<n/>• <c val="#TooltipNumbers">Lost Vikings, Rexxar, Samuro, and Nova:</c> Boons and curses apply to all controlled units/clones.<n/>• <c val="#TooltipNumbers">Abathur:</c> Boons and curses apply to Symbiote (NOT the host) and Ultimate Evolution.<n/>• <c val="#TooltipNumbers">Cho'Gall:</c> Cho and Gall share all boons and curses.<n/><n/><c val="FFDE36">Golden Heroes:</c> These Heroes have access to unique boons and curses, in addition to the generic ones that all Heroes have access to.<n/><n/><c val="#TooltipNumbers">Boons:</c> Obtained when reaching level <c val="#TooltipNumbers">1</c>, <c val="#TooltipNumbers">4</c>, <c val="#TooltipNumbers">7</c>, and <c val="#TooltipNumbers">10</c>. If you win early and skip any boons, those skipped boons will be offered in the next game, up to the level <c val="#TooltipNumbers">10</c> boons. Completing a map objective (for example getting three tributes on Cursed Hollow) also replaces a future curse with a boon, once per game. Boons can be <c val="ffffff">Common</c>, <c val="00ff55">Uncommon</c>, <c val="0070dd">Rare</c>, <c val="a335ee">Epic</c>, or <c val="ff8000">Legendary</c>, increasing in rarity and power. You are guaranteed at least a <c val="0070dd">Rare Boon</c> at the start of every game, and a <c val="ff8000">Legendary Boon</c> every <c val="#TooltipNumbers">4</c> games.<n/><n/><c val="01ffff">Milestone Boons:</c> These are boons with unique effects that are obtained when reaching round <c val="#TooltipNumbers">7</c> on any difficulty as a milestone reward.<n/><n/><c val="e6cc80">Starter Boons:</c> These are potent boons that can only be obtained at the start of the game. Some boons only show up with specific starter boons.<n/><n/><c val="c52020">Curses:</c> Obtained every time the enemy Team reaches a talent tier, as well as level <c val="#TooltipNumbers">24</c>, <c val="#TooltipNumbers">27</c>, and <c val="#TooltipNumbers">30</c>. If you win early and skip any curses, those skipped curses will be offered in the next game, up to the level <c val="#TooltipNumbers">10</c> curses.<n/><n/><c val="A84987">Mythic Curses:</c> These are powerful and unique curses that show up at the start of the game and every <c val="#TooltipNumbers">4</c> games afterwards on <c val="#TooltipNumbers">Mythic</c> difficulty.<n/><n/><c val="9B2400">Challenge Curses:</c> These are curses that can be difficult to deal with but offer great rewards.<n/><n/><c val="FFDE36">Rerolls:</c> Obtain <c val="#TooltipNumbers">3</c> rerolls at the start of a run and <c val="#TooltipNumbers">1</c> additional reroll at the start of every subsequent game which can be used to reroll the selection of boons and curses. Boon rerolls and curse rerolls are separate.<n/><n/><c val="FC4747">Bans:</c> Obtain <c val="#TooltipNumbers">3</c> bans at the start of a run and <c val="#TooltipNumbers">1</c> additional ban at the start of every subsequent game which can be used to ban boons and curses, preventing them from showing up for the rest of the run. Infinite boons/curses, starter boons, mythic curses, and milestone boons cannot be banned.
